### [177] [Compile by Training: Turning Natural-Language Specifications into Local Neural Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04199)
*Yuntian Deng,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出“编译即训练”（compile by training）方法，将自然语言规范转化为可复用的神经函数。通过教师模型生成示例训练小型适配器，实现高精度语义匹配，但牺牲了部分编译速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统规则难以描述复杂文本函数的问题，同时避免每次调用大型远程模型带来的高成本、高延迟及供应商依赖问题。

Method: 在编译阶段，利用教师模型生成特定任务示例，用于训练一个紧凑解释器的小型适配器（adapter）。最终生成的函数无需教师模型即可运行，并支持存储、版本控制和组合。

Result: 在FuzzyBench-Hard数据集上达到83.6%的语义准确率，优于快速编译器。代价是编译时间较长（约一分钟而非几秒）。成功部署于多站点网站助手、语言控制的3D化身及英-克劳迪什语双向翻译器等场景。

Conclusion: 该方法有效平衡了功能实现的灵活性与执行效率，证明了将自然语言规范转化为高效、独立运行的神经函数的可行性。

Abstract: Many recurring text functions are easy to describe but difficult to implement with rules, while calling a large remote model for every input introduces repeated cost, latency, and dependency on a provider. We present compile by training, which turns a natural-language specification into a reusable neural function. At compile time, teacher models generate task-specific examples that are used to train a small adapter for a compact interpreter. The resulting function runs without the teachers and can be stored, versioned, and composed like ordinary software. On FuzzyBench-Hard, a subset on which the Program-as-Weights fast compiler produced no exact matches, compile by training reaches 83.6% semantic accuracy. This higher accuracy comes with a higher compile-time cost: roughly a minute rather than seconds for the fast compiler. We deploy the compiler in a public interactive service and demonstrate compiled functions in a multi-site website helper, a language-controlled 3D avatar, and a bidirectional English-Claudish translator.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Practical Threshold-based Tree Edit Distance Lower-Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03078)
*Lukáš Moravec,Radim Bača*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对基于树编辑距离（TED）的阈值相似性搜索中现有下界方法在剪枝精度与计算成本间的权衡问题，本文通过加速SED下界并引入结合结构约束的SED-struct过滤器，显著提升了异构树集合中的剪枝效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TED下界方法存在根本性权衡：低成本的统计和结构界限剪枝能力有限，而精确的遍历式字符串编辑距离（SED）界限计算成本高；且缺乏对近期结构过滤器和阈值感知SED实现的全面比较。

Method: 1. 对最先进的TED下界进行全面的实验比较；2. 利用Ukkonen有界字符串编辑距离算法加速SED下界的计算；3. 提出SED-struct阈值过滤器，通过轴感知约束增强SED以捕捉节点间的结构关系。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，SED-struct在保持实际过滤成本的同时，始终实现了最高的过滤精度，特别是在标准SED下界精度较低的异构树集合中效果显著。

Conclusion: SED-struct通过结合加速的SED下界和结构约束，有效解决了剪枝精度与计算成本之间的权衡问题，为树结构数据的相似性搜索提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Threshold-based similarity search over tree-structured data using tree edit distance (TED) is computationally intensive. Given a query tree and a database of trees, the goal is to retrieve all trees within a predefined TED threshold $τ$. Because exact TED computation is expensive, practical methods employ lower-bounds to prune dissimilar candidates before verification. Existing lower-bounds exhibit a fundamental trade-off: inexpensive statistical and structural bounds provide limited pruning power, whereas the more precise traversal-based string edit distance (SED) bound is expensive to compute using standard quadratic dynamic programming. Moreover, previous comparative studies do not cover recent structural filters or threshold-aware SED implementations, leaving their practical trade-offs unclear. In this article, we first provide a comprehensive experimental comparison of state-of-the-art TED lower-bounds in terms of pruning precision and computational cost. We then accelerate the SED lower-bound using Ukkonen's bounded string edit distance algorithm, substantially reducing its runtime without affecting its pruning power. Finally, we introduce the SED-struct threshold filter, which strengthens SED with axes-aware constraints capturing structural relationships among tree nodes. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that SED-struct consistently achieves the highest filtering precision while retaining practical filtering costs. The results suggest that SED-struct is particularly beneficial for heterogeneous tree collections in which the standard SED lower-bound achieves relatively low precision.

</details>


### [179] [What Happens When the Model Eats the Stack? Rethinking the Research Agenda for Data Agents to Withstand the Bitter Lesson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03141)
*Liana Patel,Siddharth Jha,Negar Arabzadeh,Carlos Guestrin,Ion Stoica,Matei Zaharia*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 面对大语言模型（LLMs）快速内化传统数据代理能力的“痛苦教训”，本文主张未来的数据系统应将重点从补偿模型局限转向支持“持久语义上下文”。研究指出，通过提供精心策划的环境背景信息，可显著提升数据代理性能，但这要求数据系统将持久语义上下文作为一等公民抽象进行原生支持，并提出了相应的数据结构、存储及一致性协议等研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受“痛苦教训”驱动，即端到端训练的LLMs正在迅速掌握以往需要精心设计的数据代理才能完成的能力。现有的旨在弥补模型局限的系统层正逐渐被模型本身所取代，因此需要重新定位数据系统的研究机会。

Method: 基于实证洞察进行分析，识别出支持跨查询数据代理的“持久语义上下文”（persistent semantic context）这一核心概念。探讨了将该上下文作为一等公民抽象引入数据系统的必要性，并概述了实现该愿景所需的关键技术挑战与研究方向，包括高效上下文数据结构、存储方法、压缩技术及语义一致性协议。

Result: 发现持久语义上下文层在提升数据代理性能方面展现出巨大潜力，但同时也带来了显著的系统挑战。确立了一个关键需求：未来数据系统必须原生地服务持久语义上下文，以支持能够处理庞大复杂知识库的强大数据代理。

Conclusion: 随着模型能力的提升，数据系统的核心价值在于管理和服务于持久语义上下文。未来的研究应聚焦于设计高效的数据结构、存储机制和一致性协议，以确保存储在其中的上下文知识的完整性与正确性，从而赋能复杂场景下的数据代理工作。

Abstract: The bitter lesson poses an existential question for the data systems community, whereby large language models (LLMs) trained end-to-end are rapidly internalizing new capabilities that previously required carefully engineered data agents. Guided by empirical insights, we argue that as models continue to improve, many proposed system layers designed to compensate for model limitations on a given task will increasingly be subsumed by the model itself. We instead identify enduring research opportunities, which lie in supporting data agents across many queries with curated contextual information about the data environment, which we call persistent semantic context. We find that these context layers demonstrate strong promise for improving data agent performance, but they also raise significant system challenges. Thus, a key requirement for future data systems will lie in natively serving persistent semantic contexts as a first-class abstraction in order to enable capable data agents working over huge, complex knowledge corpora. Towards this vision, we outline exciting new research opportunities, including designing efficient context data structures, storage methods, compression techniques, and semantic consistency protocols, to ensure integrity and correctness of the stored contextual knowledge.

</details>


### [180] [Does SRL Pave the Road to Explainable Reasoning? Lessons Learned from an Implementer's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03441)
*Lander Maes,Bryan-Elliott Tam,Jitse De Smet,Jos De Roo,Pieter Colpaert,Ruben Taelman*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文实现了两个SRL（Shape Rules Language）推理引擎，评估了基于SPARQL的重用引擎和专用引擎。结果显示，专用引擎速度比SPARQL引擎快2-6倍，但基于SPARQL的构建成本更低。验证表明，尽管规范尚不成熟，SRL已具备实用推理能力，且可低成本实现符合规范的引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决RDF推理领域缺乏统一标准的问题。传统方法依赖固定的推导制度（如RDFS、OWL）、非标准的规则语言或特定实现，导致从业者缺乏构建合规引擎的指导及对其适用问题的认知。旨在通过实现和评估SRL引擎，提供实践指导并验证该语言的可行性。

Method: 1. 实现两个SRL引擎：一个是复用现有SPARQL查询引擎及其解析器的“SPARQL基础引擎”；另一个是“专用引擎”。
2. 使用SRL一致性测试套件及补充的用例驱动测试对两个引擎进行验证。
3. 在经典RDF推理任务上评估两者的正确性（soundness）、完备性（completeness）和速度。

Result: 1. 两个引擎均通过了SRL一致性测试。
2. SPARQL基础引擎利用模块化解析器和SPARQL CONSTRUCT减少开发工作量。
3. 专用引擎性能更优，速度是SPARQL引擎的2到6倍，且随着规则集规模扩大，性能差距进一步拉大。
4. 证明了可以在SPARQL引擎基础上低成本构建可用SRL引擎，虽有一定速度折损，但可通过专用实现弥补。

Conclusion: 尽管SRL规范尚不成熟，但该语言已支持实际有用的推理任务。开发者可以以较低成本在SPARQL引擎之上构建符合规范的SRL引擎，若追求高性能则可选择专用实现。本研究为SRL引擎的开发提供了实证支持和实践指南。

Abstract: The Shape Rules Language (SRL) Working Draft defines how to derive new RDF triples from an RDF graph using inference rules. Each rule matches graph patterns and instantiates triple templates whose output feeds into validation pipelines, SPARQL queries, or further inference. RDF reasoning has traditionally relied on fixed entailment regimes (RDFS, OWL), rule-based ad-hoc languages such as N3, or other implementation-specific solutions without a shared standard. SRL introduces user-defined production rules with a defined grammar, dependency analysis, execution ordering, and termination guarantees. However, no authoritative implementation exists, leaving practitioners with little guidance on how to build a conformant engine or on what problems the language can solve. We implemented two SRL engines and evaluated both on classical RDF reasoning tasks for soundness, completeness, and speed. The first reuses an existing SPARQL query engine and its query parser; the second is a dedicated engine. The SPARQL-based engine reused an existing modular parser for query construction and SPARQL CONSTRUCT for triple production, reducing engine-specific work. The dedicated engine was two to six times faster, the gap widening as rule sets grow. Both engines were validated against the SRL conformance test suite, supplemented by additional use-case-driven tests. A usable SRL engine can be built inexpensively on top of a SPARQL engine, with a moderate speed trade-off that a dedicated implementation recovers. Despite the specification's immaturity, the language already supports practically useful reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [181] [KnowFeat: Knowledge-Guided Feature Engineering via LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03529)
*Chengsong You,Wangyue Li,Weiqiao Que,Qizhou Chen,Kunyan Wu,Wei Deng,Feng Zhu,Xiaofeng He*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出KnowFeat框架，通过注入结构化领域知识并经过三阶段验证管道，解决LLM自动化特征工程缺乏严谨性和可解释性的问题，在多个基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动化特征工程方法虽然能生成有意义的特征，但缺乏结构化的领域知识、严格的验证机制以及可解释的来源追溯（provenance）。

Method: 提出KnowFeat框架：1. 将领域知识组织为五类（模式元数据、监管指标、检测规则、专家意见、法院文档证据）作为结构化上下文注入LLM智能体；2. 实施三阶段验证管道（代码执行、统计质量检查、模型有效性评估）过滤候选特征；3. 为每个被接受的特征生成来源记录，追溯其设计到具体的知识资产。

Result: 在消除特征选择泄漏的严格保留协议下，KnowFeat在十二个公共基准测试中排名第一（平均排名2.3），优于其他七种方法（单侧Wilcoxon p=0.017），在电信流失数据集上AUC峰值增益达+11.6个百分点。在真实世界比特币反洗钱（Elliptic）和合成数字货币反洗钱（SimECNY）数据集上保持有竞争力的检测性能，且具备完整的来源可追溯性。

Conclusion: KnowFeat通过结合结构化领域知识和严格的验证流程，显著提升了LLM驱动特征工程的有效性、鲁棒性和可解释性，适用于包括金融合规在内的复杂场景。

Abstract: Automated feature engineering with large language models (LLMs) can produce semantically meaningful features for tabular data, yet existing methods lack structured domain knowledge, rigorous verification, and explainable provenance. We propose KnowFeat, a knowledge-guided feature engineering framework that organizes domain knowledge into five types -- schema metadata, regulatory indicators, detection rules, expert opinions, and court document evidence -- and injects them as structured context into an LLM agent. A three-stage verification pipeline filters candidates through code execution, statistical quality checks, and model effectiveness evaluation. Every accepted feature carries a provenance record tracing its design to specific knowledge assets. Under a strict held-out protocol that eliminates feature-selection leakage, KnowFeat ranks first (avg. rank 2.3) across twelve public benchmarks among seven methods (one-sided Wilcoxon p=0.017), with a peak gain of +11.6 pp AUC on a telecom churn dataset. On a real-world Bitcoin anti-money laundering (AML) dataset (Elliptic) and a synthetic digital currency AML benchmark (SimECNY), KnowFeat maintains competitive detection performance with full provenance traceability.

</details>


### [182] [Tree Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03538)
*Nicolas Spyratos*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种基于带标签有向树的新型数据库模型，通过函数代数统一处理遍历查询和分析查询，支持继承、关系数据库兼容性及用户友好接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统关系模型中数据访问（关系代数）与数据分析（SQL Group-by）分离的问题，提供一个统一的正式框架以简化数据访问与分析。

Method: 构建以对象为根、属性为非根节点的树结构数据库；定义树操作集和基于简单函数代数（限制、组合、配对、笛卡尔积）的查询语言，将遍历查询作为分析查询的基础模块。

Result: 该模型具备强大的表达能力：无缝支持继承机制；可在树数据库之上定义一致的关系数据库（树作为语义层）；可作为用户友好的界面用于访问和分析关系数据。

Conclusion: 该树数据库模型通过将遍历查询与分析查询整合在同一形式化框架内，实现了数据访问与分析的统一，相比传统关系模型具有显著优势。

Abstract: We propose a novel database model whose basic structure is a labeled, directed tree with node identities. Intuitively, the root of the tree is seen as an object (or entity), the non-root nodes as attributes of the object and the semantics of each attribute is represented by the unique path leading from the root to the attribute. We define a tree database to be a set of such trees. The trees of the database can be combined to produce new trees using a set of operations on trees that we define in the paper. The query language of our model offers two types of queries, traversal queries and analytic queries. A query (whether traversal or analytic) is always defined over a tree, which is either a tree in the database or a tree derived from other trees using tree operations. The operations on trees and the query language are both defined using a simple functional algebra whose operations are: restriction of a function, composition of functions, pairing of functions and Cartesian product of sets.
  A distinctive feature of our model is that traversal queries and analytic queries are both defined within the same formal framework; and in fact, traversal queries serve as the building blocks for analytic queries. This is in sharp contrast to the relational model, where analytic queries are defined outside the relational algebra, in the form of SQL Group-by queries. Therefore our model supports data access and data analysis within the same formal framework.
  We demonstrate the expressive power of our model by showing: (a) how our model can support inheritance in a seamless manner, (b) how one can define consistent relational databases on top of a tree database - with the tree database playing the role of an underlying semantic layer and (c) how a tree database can be used as a user-friendly interface for accessing and analyzing relational data.

</details>


### [183] [Convolution Sum-Product Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03672)
*Kyle Deeds,Timo Camillo Merkl,Dan Suciu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出卷积和积查询（CSPQs），一种允许变量线性组合的扩展。通过引入秩分析、商空间和变量替换等线性代数技术，改进了最坏情况最优连接和树分解算法，实现了对CSPQs的高效评估，并证明了该方法适用于任意域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的处理线性不等/相等约束的技术在评估CSPQs时渐近次优。由于CSPQs在实际应用（如图像处理）和理论问题（如$(\min,+)$-卷积和$k$-SUM猜想）中具有重要意义，需要开发更高效的评估算法。

Method: 1. 将最坏情况最优连接（Worst-Case Optimal Joins）适配到CSPQs；2. 将树分解（TDs）扩展到CSPQs，其中线性组合作为“一等公民”参与分解；3. 定义三种宽度度量以限制执行复杂度；4. 利用线性代数技术（秩分析、商空间、变量替换）优化执行过程。

Result: 提出了针对CSPQs的多种评估算法，其运行时效率类似于合取查询。定义了基于线性代数的树分解扩展及相应的三种宽度度量，能够根据域值大小、活动域大小和关系支持度来界定复杂度。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法不仅解决了CSPQs评估中的次优问题，还展示了这些技术可以应用于有理数域之外的任意域，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We study query evaluation for an extension of sum-product queries (SPQ) that allows atoms with linear combinations of variables (e.g. $A(2X+3Y-Z, Y+Z)$), which we call convolution sum-product queries (CSPQs). These queries arise in both practical settings (e.g. image processing workloads) and theoretical ones (e.g. the $(\min,+)$-convolution and $k$-SUM conjectures), and capture SPQs with added linear equality constraints. While prior work has considered the impact of linear in- and dis-equality constraints on query evaluation, the techniques developed in that setting are asymptotically sub-optimal for CSPQs. To address this, we describe several evaluation algorithms for CSPQs that leverage linear algebra techniques like rank analysis, quotient spaces, and variable substitution. First, we adapt Worst-Case Optimal Joins to CSPQs, achieving a runtime similar in spirit to that for conjunctive queries. Then, we extend the definition of tree decompositions (TDs) to CSPQs, and describe a factorized execution. In this extension, linear combinations are first-class citizens and play the same role as variables in traditional TDs. We define three width measures that bound the complexity of this execution with respect to the size of the domain values, of the active domain, and of the relation's support. Lastly, we show how these methods can be applied to arbitrary fields beyond the rationals.

</details>


### [184] [Aker: Density-Aware Approximate Caching for Vector Search (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03712)
*Sukjoon Oh,Minki Kang,Dohyun Kim,Baotong Lu,Jing Liu,Qianxi Zhang,Qi Chen,Youjip Won*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出Aker，一种用于基于磁盘的近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）的近似缓存系统。通过动态调整每查询相似度阈值适应局部密度，以及采用del-consistency一致性模型处理向量更新，显著提升了召回率和吞吐量，同时降低了内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有基于磁盘的ANNS系统中，因频繁磁盘访问导致的高I/O开销问题；克服现有近似缓存方法在应对高维空间变化局部邻居密度时无法兼顾高吞吐与高准确度的局限，以及缺乏有效机制来维持向量更新下缓存正确性的不足。

Method: 1. 引入每查询相似度阈值：每个缓存条目维护独立阈值，并根据观察到的缓存命中模式动态调整，以适应不同的局部邻居密度。
2. 提出del-consistency一致性模型：对删除操作采用急切应用（eagerly），对插入操作采用延迟应用（lazily），从而实施低开销的刷新机制以限制缓存陈旧度并保证准确性。
3. 将Aker集成到pgvector中进行评估。

Result: 在代表性工作负载上，Aker相比先前解决方案召回率最高提升64个百分点，QPS（每秒查询数）最高提升3.2倍，且仅使用pgvector共享缓冲区0.6倍的内存。

Conclusion: Aker通过自适应阈值和新的缓存一致性模型，有效解决了磁盘ANNS中缓存效率、准确性及更新维护的问题，实现了性能的大幅优化。

Abstract: Disk-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS) incurs high I/O overhead due to frequent disk accesses during index traversal. Approximate caching, which reuses the results of past queries to serve future similar queries, offers a promising approach to bypass expensive disk searches. However, existing approaches suffer from two key limitations. First, their approximate hit predicates fail to simultaneously achieve high throughput and high accuracy, as they do not adapt to the varying local neighbor density in high-dimensional spaces. Second, they lack an effective refresh mechanism to maintain cache correctness under vector updates.
  We present Aker, an approximate cache for disk-based ANNS. Aker addresses these limitations through two core design choices. First, we introduce a per-query similarity threshold, where each cache entry maintains its own threshold that is dynamically adjusted based on observed cache hit patterns. This design enables Aker to adapt to neighborhood densities to preserve both efficiency and accuracy. Second, we propose del-consistency, a consistency model for ANNS caches that applies deletions eagerly and insertions lazily. Under this model, Aker implements a low-overhead refresh mechanism that bounds cache staleness and preserves high search accuracy. We integrate Aker into pgvector and evaluate it on representative workloads. Aker improves recall by up to 64 percentage points over prior solutions and increases QPS by up to 3.2x, while using 0.6x the memory of pgvector's shared buffers.

</details>


### [185] [From Data Querying to Data Investigations: Rethinking Natural Language Interfaces for Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03898)
*Fabian Wenz,Zixuan Chen,Carsten Binnig*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出“数据调查”新范式，旨在解决传统Text-to-SQL仅支持单次查询的局限。通过原型系统D^2，实现基于序列SQL查询的自主搜索、推理与证据支持决策，显著扩展了数据库自然语言接口的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Text-to-SQL范式假设用户问题可由单个SQL查询回答，但实际场景中用户需要解决复杂的数据问题，这要求系统能够执行一系列SQL查询并基于中间结果进行推理，而非仅仅运行单一查询。

Method: 引入“数据调查”（data investigations）新范式，开发原型系统D^2。该系统具备自主搜索、对中间结果进行推理以及收集数据以解决数据问题的能力。研究还构建了一个基于“谋杀之谜”（Murder Mystery）数据集的新基准测试。

Result: 在基于谋杀之谜数据集的新基准上，D^2展示了其在需要数据调查任务中的潜力，能够做出有证据支持的决策，其能力超越了传统单查询问答系统的局限。

Conclusion: 传统的Text-to-SQL范式存在局限性，无法有效应对需要多步推理和中间结果分析的复杂数据问题。提出的“数据调查”范式及D^2系统证明了通过序列SQL查询和自主推理来解决复杂数据问题的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Natural language (NL) interfaces to databases have been optimized for the wrong problem. The dominant Text-to-SQL paradigm assumes that users ask questions that can be answered by single SQL queries. In practice, however, users seek assistance with solving data problems. This requires searching a database by sequences of SQL queries while reasoning over intermediate results instead of just running one SQL query. This paper therefore introduces a new paradigm for NL interfaces to data, which we call data investigations. We present D^2, a first prototype of a data investigation system that embodies this vision by autonomously searching, reasoning over, and collecting data to solve data problems. Using a newly constructed benchmark based on the Murder Mystery dataset, we demonstrate the potential of D^2 for tasks that require data investigations with evidence-backed decisions, extending beyond the capabilities of traditional single-query question answering.

</details>


### [186] [Property Testing for Recursive Query Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03908)
*Isolde Adler,Carsten Lutz,Quentin Manière,Marcin Przybyłko,Lukas Schulze*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据库查询的属性测试问题，重点在于递归查询语言（如2RPQs和Monadic Datalog）。主要发现包括：所有2RPQ的非答案均可通过常数次查询以单侧误差进行测试；对于受限制的Monadic Datalog程序也有类似结果；而对于不受限的程序，若不等价于α-无环程序，则不可测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关于布尔合取查询属性测试的结果（Chen and Yoshida, 2019）仅适用于非递归查询。为了扩展这一理论框架，需要研究更复杂的递归查询语言的属性可测试性条件。

Method: 作者通过分析两种主要的递归查询语言——双向正则路径查询（2RPQs）和Monadic Datalog，结合α-无环性的概念，利用完成查询（completion queries）和大小查询（size queries）来评估其属性可测试性。

Result: 1. 任何2RPQ的非答案都是常数查询可测试的（单侧误差）。
2. 在递归限制为线性且规则体为α-无环的情况下，特定类Monadic Datalog程序也是可测试的。
3. 如果Monadic Datalog程序不等价于α-无环程序（假设规则体自连接自由），则其虚假性不是常数查询可测试的。
4. 存在某些具有α-无环规则体但非严格线性的程序，仍具备常数查询可测试性。

Conclusion: 属性测试的可判定性与查询是否等价于α-无环结构密切相关。虽然部分递归查询（如2RPQs）保持了良好的可测试性，但不受限制的Monadic Datalog中非α-无环结构的不可测试性揭示了递归复杂度对测试效率的根本限制。

Abstract: In the context of database querying, property testing provides a framework for testing query answers with high confidence while inspecting only a sublinear part of the database, through completion queries and size queries. A fundamental result of Chen and Yoshida (2019) states that non-satisfaction of a Boolean conjunctive query $q$ is testable with a constant number of such queries and one-sided error if and only if $q$ is equivalent to an $α$-acyclic query. In this article, we initiate the study of property testing for recursive query languages, focusing on two-way regular path queries (2RPQs) and monadic Datalog. One of our main results is positive: non-answers to any 2RPQ are constant query testable with one-sided error. We extend this slightly to a certain class of monadic Datalog programs in which recursion is restricted to be linear and rule bodies must be $α$-acyclic. Turning towards unrestricted monadic Datalog, we next show that if a monadic Datalog program $Π$ is not equivalent to an $α$-acyclic program, then falsity of $Π$ is not constant query testable with one-sided error. This is under the assumption that all rule-bodies are self-join free. We leave open the case of monadic Datalog programs with $α$-acyclic rule bodies that are not restricted to linear recursion, but observe as a first step that there exist $α$-acyclic programs that are mildly non-linear and constant query testable with one-sided error.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Structure and Implementation of New Practical English Textbooks Driven by Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02981)
*Ya Wang,Lei Zhang,Xueguang Yang,Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出并验证了一种由人工智能驱动的实用英语教材原型。该教材采用五层架构，在186名非英语专业本科生中进行了为期八周的测试。结果显示，与静态数字教材相比，该系统显著提高了单元完成准确率（72.4%至84.9%）、口语任务平均分（提升10.8分），并减少了教师批改时间（31.6%）。结论表明，AI驱动教材能在保持课程稳定性的同时，提供个性化学习路径和可追溯的课堂数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能技术的发展，应用英语材料的形式正从固定的纸质序列转变为能够诊断学习者、推荐任务和提供形成性反馈的自适应学习系统。旨在探索这种新形态教材的结构及其在实际教学中的应用效果。

Method: 提出了一个包含知识图谱、学习者画像、任务生成、反馈编排和教师端治理的五层架构。构建了一个原型系统，并在186名非英语专业本科生中进行了为期八周的教学实验，将其表现与传统的静态数字教材进行对比分析。

Result: 实验数据显示，使用AI驱动教材的系统在以下方面优于静态数字教材：单元完成准确率从72.4%提升至84.9%；口语任务平均得分提高10.8分；教师批改作业的时间减少了31.6%。

Conclusion: AI驱动的英语教材能够在维持课程结构稳定性的基础上，有效提供个性化的学习路径、丰富的练习材料以及可追溯的课堂教学数据，从而优化教学效果并减轻教师负担。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is changing the form of applied English materials from fixed paper sequences to adaptive learning systems that can diagnose learners, recommend tasks, and provide formative feedback. This paper studies the structure and application of a new practical English textbook driven by artificial intelligence. A five-layer architecture is proposed: knowledge mapping, learner profiling, task generation, feedback orchestration, and teacher-side governance. A prototype was tested on 186 non-English-major undergraduates for eight weeks of teaching. Compared with a static digital textbook, the proposed system increased the unit completion accuracy from 72.4% to 84.9%, raised the average score for speaking tasks by 10.8 points, and reduced the teacher's correction time by 31.6%. Therefore, an AI-driven textbook can maintain the stability of the curriculum while providing personalised learning paths, rich practice materials and traceable classroom data.

</details>


### [188] [MasterControl Seventeen Every Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03209)
*MasterControl AI Lab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了一种“治理型”企业分析架构，结合语言模型（LLM）的自然语言理解能力与确定性策略执行预批准的分析程序。该方法在保持表达力的同时确保了结果的可复现性和证据完整性。实验表明，相比完全由LLM进行运行时规划的方法，该混合架构在特定配置下表现更优且稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统基于大语言模型的企业分析中存在的不可控、结果不可复现以及缺乏完整证据链的问题。旨在通过引入治理机制，平衡灵活性（LLM的意图理解）与安全性/可靠性（确定性策略执行）。

Method: 提出一种分层架构：1) LLM负责解释用户问题并提取意图；2) 确定性策略引擎根据固定规则选择并运行预批准的 analytical program（支持关系运算、聚合、比较、窗口函数、排序和相似度计算）。这种设计确保了语义、策略、数据和执行规则的固定性。

Result: 在440次运行实验中对比了不同模型和架构：三个8B参数量的模型在生成SQL和运行时选择工具方面，有330个运行时规划片段未能完全满足“答案+证据”的合同要求；而采用Qwen3-8B仅用于意图解释、由策略引擎执行已批准程序的架构，在110次测试中全部成功匹配目标。

Conclusion: 这种受限的治理方法在定义的分析类内仍具有足够的表达能力，并能保证结果的可复现性。虽然这是特定配置下的结果，不能证明其他设计的运行时智能体无法成功，但它证明了通过分离意图理解和确定性强执行，可以有效提升企业级分析的可靠性和合规性。

Abstract: We study a governed approach to enterprise analytics: a language model interprets the question, while deterministic policy selects and runs a pre-approved analytical program that returns both results and evidence. We show that this restriction can remain expressive within a defined analytical class, using relational operations plus aggregation, comparison, windows, ranking, and similarity. Fixed meaning, policy, data, and execution rules also make results replayable. Across 440 runs, three 8B models generated SQL and selected tools at runtime, while Qwen3-8B interpreted intent only and policy executed the approved program. None of 330 runtime-planning episodes matched the full answer-and-evidence contract across all test datasets; the policy-executed analyzer matched 110 of 110. This is a configuration-specific result, not evidence that runtime agents cannot succeed under other designs.

</details>


### [189] [Speculative Macro Commit for Faster Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03236)
*Zeyu Liu,Souvik Kundu,Peter A. Beerel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出推测宏提交（SMC）机制，通过两层级智能体系统（权威模型+推测草稿模型）复用多步动作链，显著降低LLM智能体的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具使用型LLM智能体在串行动作-观察循环中耗时严重，单步推测无法充分利用并行性，导致整体延迟较高。

Method: 构建两-tier架构：大型权威演员模型生成官方轨迹，快速推测草稿模型在隔离环境中预测并执行未来动作链。从训练轨迹中提取重复的多动作骨架存入宏库，运行时匹配草稿预测；若演员的下一步调用与草稿首动作匹配，则提交剩余已执行的草稿步骤及观测结果至官方轨迹。

Result: 基于Qwen3.5系列模型，在$τ^2$-Bench Telecom子集上，SMC保持准确率的同时，相比推测动作（SA）基线延迟降低10.23%，相比串行执行降低18.59%；在AppWorld上，相比SA基线减少7.7%墙钟时间，相比串行执行减少44.9%（伴随微小任务完成率下降）。

Conclusion: SMC提供了一种实用的方法，通过重用多步推测执行，实现了超越单步推测的智能体延迟优化。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents spend wall-clock time not only on model inference but also in serial action--observation turns, where each tool call, environment transition, and observation can delay subsequent decisions. We introduce \textbf{Speculative Macro Commit} (SMC), a runtime mechanism for a two-tier agent system: a large authoritative actor model produces the official trajectory, while a faster speculative drafter model continuously predicts and executes future action chains on an isolated environment snapshot. SMC mines recurring multi-action skeletons from training traces and stores them in a macro library used to match against action chains predicted by the drafter at runtime. When the actor's next tool call matches the first drafted action, SMC commits the remaining pre-executed draft steps, together with their observations, to the official trajectory. Using Qwen3.5-27B INT4 as the authoritative actor model and Qwen3.5-4B as the speculative drafter model, SMC matches the sequential agent's overall accuracy while reducing latency by 10.23\% over the Speculative Actions (SA) baseline and 18.59\% over sequential execution on the $τ^2$-Bench Telecom subset. On AppWorld, SMC reduces wall time by 7.7\% over SA baseline and 44.9\% over sequential execution, with a small reduction in task completion. Overall, SMC provides a practical way to reuse multi-step speculative execution and reduce agent latency beyond single-step speculative actions. Our code is publicly available \href{https://github.com/zeyuliu1037/speculative-macro-commit}{\textcolor{magenta}{here}}.

</details>


### [190] [Fresh Memory, Stale Plans: Dependency-Scoped Validation for Distributed LLM-Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03340)
*Evan Chen,Shiqiang Wang,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PlanFence协议，解决分布式LLM智能体团队中的“过时计划执行”问题。通过依赖范围的动作验证，确保执行器仅验证影响当前动作的相关记录，从而在动态环境中保证行动的有效性，避免基于陈旧信息采取行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式大语言模型（LLM）智能体团队中，存在“过时计划执行”（stale-plan execution）现象：尽管状态是最新的，但智能体可能仍依据过时的计划行动。例如，一个智能体根据需求$r_3$制定计划，另一个智能体提交新需求$r_4$，但执行者收到$r_4$后并未替换原计划，导致基于$r_3$的过时计划被继续执行。状态的新鲜度并不能保证授权行动的计划的合法性。

Method: 引入PlanFence，这是一种基于依赖范围的动作验证协议。具体机制包括：1) 计划引用其使用的确切公共记录；2) 执行器仅验证那些可能影响待处理外部动作的记录；3) 当验证不完整时，进行重新规划或阻塞等待。

Result: 在30个包含计划修订控制的实时工作流实验中：1) 仅依赖状态新鲜度的执行器在所有任务中都执行了过时计划；2) PlanFence完成了所有任务，未执行任何无效动作。控制回放揭示了两个条件边界：低变更率下主动同步可降低协调停滞；高变更率下PlanFence避免了重复更新路径协调；共享键空间增大时，PlanFence避免了验证无关状态。

Conclusion: PlanFence提供了受控的安全性和系统成本优化结果，而非通用的任务准确性提升。它有效解决了分布式智能体系统中的计划过时问题，确保了行动与最新相关状态的一致性。

Abstract: Distributed LLM-agent teams can read the latest shared facts and still act on an obsolete plan. A planner may derive an action from requirement $r_3$, another agent may commit $r_4$, and an executor may receive $r_4$ without replacing the plan derived from $r_3$. We call this \emph{stale-plan execution}: state freshness does not establish that the plan authorizing an action remains valid. We introduce PlanFence, a dependency-scoped action-validation protocol. Plans cite the exact public records they used, and an executor validates only the records that can affect the pending external action, replanning once or blocking when validation is incomplete. In 30 controlled live workflows with a post-plan revision, a freshness-only executor acts on the obsolete plan in every task, whereas PlanFence completes all tasks without an invalid action. Controlled replay reveals two conditional boundaries: proactive synchronization yields lower coordination stall at low churn, while PlanFence avoids repeated update-path coordination as churn grows and avoids validating unrelated state as the shared keyspace grows. These are controlled safety and systems-cost results, not general task-accuracy gains.

</details>


### [191] [A Prompt-Engineering Approach to Develop Scalable, Flexible, and Real-Time Hybrid Micro-Level Personalization in a General Purpose AI Teaching Assistant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03402)
*Saptarshi Basu,Sandeep Kakar,Ashok Goel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于提示工程的框架，通过整合学习者特定维度（如自我评估、抽象偏好等）和布鲁姆分类法认知复杂度分析，对通用LLM/RAG教学助手进行个性化适配，无需重新训练模型即可实现自适应教育支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的由大语言模型（LLM）驱动的人工智能教学助手虽然具备可扩展性，但往往缺乏足够的个性化能力，难以满足不同学习者的多样化需求。

Method: 构建一个基于提示工程的框架，利用六个学习者特定维度生成96种 distinct 学习者画像，并结合布鲁姆分类法分析学生查询的认知复杂度。将这些属性编码为结构化提示词以引导LLM输出，并通过NLP指标实验和五人参与的人类研究进行评估。

Result: 实验结果显示，在不同个性化条件下，AI助手的回复风格和内容结构存在显著差异；统计分析证实了学习者属性与可测量的回复变化之间存在关联。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了初步证据，表明基于提示的个性化方法能够有效支持LLM驱动的教育代理实现自适应行为，从而提升个性化教育支持的效果。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) teaching assistants powered by large language models (LLMs) offer scalable educational support but often provide limited personalization. This study presents a prompt-engineering-based framework for personalizing general-purpose LLM/RAG-based AI teaching assistants such as Jill Watson across academic disciplines and courses. The framework adapts responses using six learner-specific dimensions: self-assessment, abstraction preference, verbosity preference, perceptual orientation, information processing style, and level of understanding, yielding 96 distinct learner profiles. Student queries are additionally analyzed using Bloom's Taxonomy to estimate cognitive complexity at the interaction level. Learner attributes and cognitive assessments are encoded in structured prompts that condition the LLM without requiring model retraining. The framework is evaluated through experiments using NLP metrics and a human study with five participants. Results show perceived differences in response style and structure across personalization conditions, with statistical analyses identifying learner attributes associated with measurable response changes. These findings provide preliminary evidence that prompt-based personalization can support adaptive behavior in LLM-powered educational agents.

</details>


### [192] [Caught in the Story: Narrative Captivity in Multi-turn LLMs Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03407)
*Yuhe Wu,Guangyu Wang,Yujie Chen,Jiatong Zhang,Yuran Chen,Yutong Zhang,Xiyin Cheng,Wenpeng Cao,Zhuang Liu,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“叙事囚禁”现象，指出LLM在多轮道德咨询中易受单方面叙述影响而丧失独立判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多基于单轮判断或对抗性反驳，未能反映现实中一方自我辩护、信息不对称的多轮道德咨询场景，需探究无对立立场的叙述是否会导致模型判断偏差。

Method: 构建包含5,078个跨六类道德维度的 interpersonal-conflict 场景基准；评估17种大语言模型在多轮叙事下的表现；分析偏好优化及推理时策略的影响。

Result: “叙事囚禁”现象普遍存在：相比单轮基线，多轮叙述导致最终判决平均偏移25个百分点；偏好优化是主要成因，四种推理时策略仅能部分缓解。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在真实咨询场景中难以保持独立判断，亟需改进以应对单方面叙事带来的认知偏差。

Abstract: People increasingly turn to large language models (LLMs) for everyday advice, making ethically charged interpersonal problems a practical moral-advisory context. Most prior work has studied this context through single-turn judgments or pressure-laden rebuttals, assumptions that poorly match how guidance is sought in real-world contexts. These assumptions leave unclear whether narration alone, without an explicit opposing position, can shift model judgments during multi-turn moral consultation. Yet real-world moral-conflict conversation often elicits one party's self-justifying account, which can unfold over multiple turns and create information asymmetry. We introduce \textbf{narrative captivity}, a failure mode in which a model treats an unopposed one-sided account as complete and aligns with the narrator's interpretation without seeking missing perspectives. To measure this phenomenon, we build a benchmark of $5{,}078$ interpersonal-conflict scenarios spanning six moral dimensions. Across 17 LLMs, narrative captivity is widespread: end-state judgments under multi-turn narration shift by 25 percentage points on average beyond the matched single-turn baseline. Stage-level analysis identifies preference optimization as a major contributor, while four inference-time strategies provide only partial mitigation. We hope our project fosters LLM advisors that preserve independent judgment in real-world consultation.

</details>


### [193] [Dude: A Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for Paper-Code Discrepancy Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03416)
*Weijie Liu,Running Zhao,Wenhao Yuan,Jinfeng Xu,Zhanfeng Xu,Xiaoxi Zhang,Edith Cheuk-Han Ngai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Dude，首个用于论文-代码差异检测的双检测多智能体系统，通过粒度对齐协商和两阶段显著性过滤机制解决误报问题，显著提升召回率和F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着研究提交量超出人工审查能力，LLM赋能的论文-代码差异检测受到关注。但现有单智能体LLM范式受限于上下文容量和单一侧的检测能力，导致差异检测的召回性能低下。

Method: 提出Dude（Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System），针对论文语言与代码语言间的粒度不对称导致的过度解释和过度报告挑战，设计了粒度对齐协商机制和两阶段显著性过滤机制，以防止智能体错误报告差异。

Result: 在真实世界的论文-代码差异数据集上实验表明，Dude相比基线方法，召回率和精确率最高提升22.8%，F1分数最高提升18.7%。

Conclusion: Dude通过改进的多智能体协作机制有效解决了粒度不对称带来的误报问题，显著提升了论文-代码差异检测的性能。

Abstract: LLM-empowered paper-code discrepancy detection has received growing concern since the scaling of research submissions exceeds the manual review capability. However, the limited context capacity and one-sided discrepancy detection of existing single-agent LLM paradigms lead to an inferior recall performance in detecting discrepancies. In this paper, we propose Dude, the first Dual-Detection Multi-Agent System for paper-code discrepancy detection. We discover that the granularity asymmetry of the paper-language and code-language introduces over-interpretation and over-reporting challenges in a multi-agent system design for discrepancy detection, resulting in increasing false positives. To address this, we propose a granularity-aligned negotiation and a two-stage salience-filtering mechanism in Dude, which effectively prevents agents from falsely reporting discrepancies. Experimental results in real-world paper-code discrepancy datasets showcase Dude's significant recall and precision improvement by up to 22.8%, increasing F1 score by up to 18.7% compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [194] [DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval: Evaluating Implicit Instruction Following in Full-Duplex Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03423)
*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval)，一个用于评估全双工语音代理在实时口语交互中隐性指令遵循能力的基准测试。研究发现，不同架构的模型在显式指令与角色隐含行为之间存在权衡：全双工模型对指令形式敏感，而某些多模态模型虽能保持角色一致性但缺乏主动行为的灵活性，且难以解决安全冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全双工语音代理基准测试主要依赖显式的对话管理指令，但实际部署中的代理通常通过角色或人格配置，其行为需从角色中推断。现有研究未能充分评估这种隐性指令遵循能力及其在实时交互中的表现。

Method: 提出DSB-IFEval基准，包含1,038个测试案例，涵盖8种不同的助手角色。评估五种指令遵循条件协议：默认行为、显式行为指令、角色隐含行为、角色-规则组合条件及指令冲突。使用确定性指令遵循得分（IAS）衡量实时地板管理，使用LLM判定的角色遵循得分（PAS）评估内容一致性。在六个实时语音系统上进行实验对比。

Result: 发现架构依赖性权衡：全双工模型（如F-Actor和PersonaPlex）对指令形式敏感，仅在角色条件下遵循度下降9.7%和4.5%；GPT-Realtime、MiniCPM-o和Fun-Audio-Chat强于角色内容一致，但在显式与角色指令间缺乏主动行为适应力。此外，即使系统能遵循与安全规范冲突的角色指令，仍难以在安全冲突中覆盖这些指令。

Conclusion: 从角色推断隐含行为、在适当时机执行该行为以及解决竞争性指令仍是全双工语音代理面临的独特挑战。

Abstract: Full-duplex voice agents must continuously decide when to listen, backchannel, interrupt, handle speech overlaps, take the floor, and yield. Existing benchmarks largely test these behaviors through explicit turn-management instructions, while deployed agents are often configured through roles or personas from which the appropriate conversational behavior must be inferred. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-IFEval (DSB-IFEval) for evaluating implicit instruction-following in real-time spoken interaction. (DSB-IFEval) comprises 1,038 test cases spanning eight diverse assistant roles and evaluates five conditioning protocols for instruction-following: default behavior, explicit behavioral instructions, persona-implied behavior, combined persona--rule conditioning, and instruction conflict. We measure real-time floor management using a deterministic Instruction Adherence Score (IAS) and persona-consistent content using LLM-judged Persona Adherence Score (PAS). Across six real-time speech systems, we find architecture-dependent trade-offs. Full duplex models like F-Actor and PersonaPlex are more sensitive to whether conversational behavior is stated explicitly or must be inferred from a persona, with adherence dropping by 9.7% and 4.5%, respectively, under persona-only conditioning. In contrast, GPT-Realtime, MiniCPM-o, and Fun-Audio-Chat strongly adhere to persona-consistent content, but their floor behavior does not adapt across explicit and persona-only instructions and remains constrained on several proactive actions. We further find that even if systems reliably follow conflicting directives to their prescribed persona, they still struggle to override them under safety conflict. These results show that inferring the behavior implied by a role, executing it at the appropriate conversational moment, and resolving competing instructions remain distinct challenges for full-duplex voice agents.

</details>


### [195] [Do GUI Agents Know When Not to Act? Enabling Conflict-Aware Termination for Multimodal GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03438)
*Zhaoyuan Huang,Tianjie Ju,Pengzhou Cheng,Zheng Wu,Yansi Li,Chuanbiao Song,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对GUI智能体在面对不可行指令时容易盲目执行的问题，本文提出了CONFLICTGUI基准和CONFLICTGUARD框架。通过推理时的可行性验证和动作调制机制，显著提升了智能体识别冲突并终止执行的能力，同时保持了正常任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实用户常因无意错误发出不可行的自然语言指令。现有的GUI智能体往往过度顺从（overcompliance），即在存在指令内部或指令与GUI上下文冲突的情况下仍盲目执行，缺乏“何时不应行动”的判断能力，导致可靠性不足。

Method: 1. 构建CONFLICTGUI基准：涵盖指令内部冲突和指令-GUI上下文冲突，用于研究冲突感知终止。
2. 提出CONFLICTGUARD框架：一种推理时干预框架，包含两个耦合组件：
   - 可行性验证协议：引导智能体在行动前评估指令逻辑和GUI侧证据。
   - 条件动作调制机制：将智能体从过度顺从的执行模式引导至以终止为导向的行为。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的智能体上进行实验，CONFLICTGUARD显著提高了平均冲突任务的成功率，同时保持了对正常GUI任务的原有性能表现。

Conclusion: 轻量级的推理时干预能显著提升GUI智能体识别不适当执行场景的能力，使其能够避免不必要的操作，从而增强其整体能力和可靠性。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) agents are increasingly used to execute natural-language instructions on user interfaces, yet real users may issue infeasible instructions due to benign mistakes. A reliable agent should not only know how to act, but also when not to act. In this work, we introduce CONFLICTGUI, a benchmark covering instruction-internal conflicts and instruction-GUI context conflicts to study conflict-aware termination. Our evaluation reveals severe execution-biased overcompliance: agents that perform well on feasible tasks often continue to execute blindly under conflicting instructions. To mitigate this behavior, we propose CONFLICTGUARD, an inference-time framework that aligns an agent's feasibility awareness with its action generation. CONFLICTGUARD contains two coupled components: a feasibility verification protocol that guides the agent to assess instruction logic and GUI-side evidence before acting, and a conditional action modulation mechanism that steers agents from over-compliant execution into termination-oriented behavior. Experiments across five widely-used agents demonstrate that CONFLICTGUARD improves average conflict task success rate significantly, while preserving normal GUI-task performance. These results validate that a lightweight inference-time intervention can substantially boost GUI Agent's competence to identify inappropriate execution scenarios and refrain from unnecessary actions.

</details>


### [196] [Semantic Bayesian World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03834)
*Tommaso Soru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“语义贝叶斯世界模型”（SBWMs），旨在解决知识图谱（确定性断言）与大模型/智能体（概率推理）之间的根本性不匹配，将知识整合从数据管道升级为统一的概率推理架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型与知识图谱的结合仅停留在数据供给层面，未能形成统一的推理体系，因为前者基于概率而后者基于确定性事实，这种本体论层面的错配阻碍了真正的联合推理。

Method: 构建SBWM框架：以知识图谱为基底，利用本体公理约束先验分布，通过贝叶斯条件化更新信念，并允许行动对世界进行干预；同时提出实现该框架所需的技术组件，包括RDF上的信念标注、概率蕴含机制、语义校准层及跨智能体信念交换协议。

Result: 展示了SBWM在多种场景下的优势：安全代理的精确分类（如区分快递员与窃贼）、基于逻辑蕴含而非字符串频率的精算估计、语言模型难以胜任的规划任务，以及对从未被文档明确陈述数量的估算能力。

Conclusion: 未来的研究方向应聚焦于建立支持概率推理的基础设施，使智能体能够在一个共享的、进化的信念网络中进行交互、校准和协商，从而实现从静态知识库到动态世界模型的范式转变。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs describe reality in crisp assertions, while the systems now consuming them, foundation models and autonomous agents, reason natively in probabilities. We argue that this mismatch is why the integration of language models and knowledge graphs remains a data-feeding pipeline rather than a unified reasoning architecture. We envision Semantic Bayesian World Models (SBWMs): a Web that describes the world not as a database of facts but as a shared, evolving fabric of beliefs over knowledge graphs, where ontological axioms constrain priors, observations update beliefs by Bayesian conditioning, and actions intervene upon the world. We work through what an agent gains from such a model: a home-security agent deciding whether the figure at the gate is a courier or a burglar, an actuarial estimate aggregated by entailment rather than by string frequency, a planning task that language models reliably fail, and the estimation of quantities that no document has ever stated. We then set out what the community must build to make them possible: belief annotation over RDF~1.2, probabilistic entailment regimes, semantic calibration layers, and protocols by which agents that have never met can exchange, and disagree over, calibrated beliefs.

</details>


### [197] [Beyond "Made with AI": Visualizing Provenance Density to Mitigate the Transparency Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03460)
*Qing Zhang,Yifei Huang,Juyoung Lee,Thad Starner,Jun Rekimoto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“流畅性陷阱”概念，即用户因AI生成文本的流畅性而误信幻觉内容。为解决此问题，论文提出“溯源密度（Provenance Density）”可视化界面，通过展示文本中已验证声明的密度来辅助辨别真伪。实验表明该界面能显著提升用户对真相与捏造的区分能力，并指出仅靠检索密度不足，一致性否决在动态查询中起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI使高质量文本生产成本降低，传统的“流畅度即真理”的启发式判断失效。用户倾向于信任流畅的幻觉内容，并在得知内容为AI生成后贬低准确信息。现有的二元“AI生成”标签仅披露作者身份，无法提供支撑主张的证据，导致透明度不足。

Method: 1. 定义“流畅性陷阱”作为新的失败模式。
2. 提出“溯源密度”接口，一种证据可视化界面，用于展示文本中经过验证声明的密度。
3. 进行包含81名参与者的用户研究，对比使用理想化溯源密度界面与无信号组的表现。
4. 对200个样本进行技术审计，分析检索密度和一致性否决（Consistency Veto）在区分真伪中的作用。

Result: 1. 用户研究显示，使用溯源密度接口的参与者能在真相与捏造之间产生显著的辨别差距（+4.15分，Cohen's d=1.82），而无信号组无明显区分能力。
2. 技术审计发现，仅靠检索密度不足以有效区分；出乎意料的是，在动态查询中，“一致性否决”提供了主要的判别信号。

Conclusion: 随着AI生成内容与人类写作越来越难以区分，有效的透明度机制必须从简单的作者身份披露转向基于证据的可视化。溯源密度作为一种证据可视化方法，能有效帮助用户识别虚假信息。

Abstract: As generative AI makes polished prose cheap to produce, users can no longer rely on fluency as a proxy for truth. We call this failure mode the Fluency Trap: users trust fluent hallucinations while also discounting accurate content once it is disclosed as AI-generated. Binary ``Made with AI'' labels respond with authorship disclosure, but they do not show what supports a claim. We propose Provenance Density, an evidence-visualization interface that shows the density of verified claims in a text. In a user study with 81 participants, an idealized Provenance Density interface produced a large discernment gap between truth and fabrication ($+4.15$ points, $d=1.82$), whereas participants given no signal showed no detectable discrimination. A technical audit with 200 samples shows that retrieval density alone is insufficient; unexpectedly, the Consistency Veto carries most of the discriminative signal on dynamic queries. As AI-generated content becomes indistinguishable from human writing, effective transparency must move from authorship disclosure toward evidence visualization.

</details>


### [198] [AutoGraphForge: Towards Automated Graph Theory Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03478)
*Ján Pastorek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 开发并运行了AutoGraphForge自动化图论猜想-反驳-形式化-证明系统，通过多轮迭代生成6,522个新颖猜想，并利用Lean 4和神经网络证明器进行形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立一个自动化的图论研究闭环系统，涵盖从猜想生成、反例反驳到形式化验证及证明的全过程，以辅助数学发现。

Method: 1. **猜想生成**：使用Graffiti3在小型快照表上提出猜想，并通过包含559个经典关系的线性规划新颖性过滤器排除已知结果。
2. **反例测试**：将候选猜想与约348,000张图的混合数据集（包括House of Graphs、顶点数≤9的连通图穷举数据等）进行比对，利用算法搜索反例。
3. **形式化与证明**：将幸存猜想转化为Lean 4语句骨架，结合DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B和OProver-32B两个神经证明器，并在pinned mathlib4环境下进行内核级验证。

Result: 在高性能计算集群上运行多轮后，系统成功保留了6,522个未被反驳且具新颖性的猜想，其中包括非平凡的双图正则图 annihilating number 与 edge-cover number 关系（已人工证明）。管道整体通过初步健全性检查并正在集群中运行。

Conclusion: 该端到端的AutoGraphForge管道有效实现了图论猜想的自动化发现与验证，展示了结合启发式生成、大规模数据过滤、形式化方法和神经证明技术的可行性。

Abstract: We report on our ongoing project to develop a computational pipeline, AutoGraphForge, for an automated graph-theoretic conjecturing-refuting-formalizing-proving system. Conjecture generation is counterexample-guided and runs in rounds: a Graffiti3 generator proposes conjectures over a small, evolving snapshot table $T$ (initially a few hundred graphs with their computed invariants) that grows only by counterexamples to its own conjectures. A novelty filter of $559$ classical and folklore relations, closed under transitive composition and linear identity substitution, decides via a linear program whether a candidate is already implied by known results. Surviving candidates are tested against a dataset of about $348,000$ graphs, unioning the complete House of Graphs invariant export, the exhaustive census of all connected graphs on at most nine vertices, several extremal families (strongly regular, minimal Ramsey, Cayley, cages, barbells, lollipops, spiders), and random models. Counterexample-search algorithms then attack the remainder. Run for several rounds on an HPC cluster, the loop yields $6,522$ conjectures that survived the refutation dataset, the novelty filter and every active-search run -- among them nontrivial relations between the annihilation number and the edge-cover number for bipartite and regular graphs, which we prove by hand. A subsequent formalization and proving stage deterministically translates each surviving conjecture into a Lean 4 statement skeleton; every candidate proof is kernel-verified against a pinned mathlib4 and our custom invariant preamble. This stage integrates two neural provers -- DeepSeek-Prover-V2-671B (served with vLLM) and the Lean-specialised OProver-32B -- behind the independent kernel check. It is implemented end-to-end and passes initial sanity checks, with the full pipeline currently running on the cluster.

</details>


### [199] [Making Every Tool Call Count: Necessary Tool-Evidence Path Rewards for Agentic Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03493)
*Xingming Long,Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Hanqi Feng,Shaojie Zhang,Barnabas Poczos,Chao Jiang,Zhenbo Luo,Lei Jiang,Pei Fu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NTEP标注方案和NTEP-R监督机制，通过细粒度的工具-证据路径监督解决现有VLMs在复杂查询中工具调用冗余和信息提取不足的问题，显著提升8B模型在多基准测试中的搜索准确性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）在处理需要细粒度视觉细节或外部知识的复杂查询时表现不佳；现有的训练范式仅基于最终答案正确性评估工具使用，导致模型常发出冗余/偏离目标的工具调用，且难以从观测结果中提取必要信息。

Method: 1. 提出NTEP（必要工具-证据路径）标注方案，明确指定每个查询所需的必要外部证据及对应工具调用；2. 设计NTEP-R监督机制，奖励预调用意图与必要目标一致、后调用摘要与必要证据对齐的行为；3. 引入非重复目标正则化器，惩罚对已满足目标的重复调用。

Result: 在七个图像 grounded 基准测试上，8B参数的NTEP-8B模型显著提升了面向搜索的准确率和工具使用效率，验证了细粒度工具-证据路径监督的有效性。

Conclusion: 细粒度的工具-证据路径监督对于训练鲁棒的代理型VLMs具有关键价值，能有效改善证据获取和利用过程。

Abstract: Modern vision-language models (VLMs) can directly answer many image-grounded questions, yet they often struggle with complex queries requiring fine-grained visual details or external knowledge. To acquire this missing evidence, agentic VLMs invoke tools such as image cropping, image search, and text search. However, existing training paradigms primarily evaluate tool-use based on final answer correctness, leaving evidence acquisition and utilization insufficiently supervised. This leads to two critical shortcomings: (i) models frequently issue redundant or off-target tool calls that fail to gather necessary evidence, and (ii) even when appropriate tools are called, models often fail to extract the necessary information from the resulting observations. To address these limitations, we introduce the NTEP (Necessary Tool-Evidence Path), a novel annotation scheme that explicitly specifies the essential external evidence and corresponding tool calls for each query. Building upon this, we propose NTEP-R (NTEP Reward), a supervision mechanism ensuring that each tool invocation strictly advances the reasoning process toward the final solution. Specifically, our approach rewards the agent for aligning its pre-call intent with a necessary evidence-seeking goal, and for ensuring the information summarized from the post-call observation aligns with the necessary evidence. Furthermore, we introduce a non-repeated-goal regularizer to penalize redundant calls that revisit satisfied NTEP goals. Extensive evaluations on seven image-grounded benchmarks demonstrate that our 8B-parameter instantiation, NTEP-8B, significantly improves both search-oriented accuracy and tool-use efficiency within a unified three-tool framework. These results highlight the critical value of fine-grained tool-evidence path supervision for training robust agentic VLMs.

</details>


### [200] [GrowPage: On-Demand KV Budgeting for Efficient LLM Reasoning Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03494)
*Qiankun Ma,Yanjiang Zhou,Zinan Xiong,Haofei Wang,Zhen Song,Yang Xiang,Ziyao Zhang,Hairong Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GrowPage，一种按需KV预算框架，通过动态调整KV缓存容量解决长输出推理中的内存瓶颈，优于固定预算方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV压缩方法依赖预定义的每请求预算且总容量固定，无法适应推理工作中不同请求间及生成过程中显著的需求变化。

Method: 构建轻量级双时间尺度查询摘要以捕捉近期和长期注意力行为，利用相对注意力工作集估计需求演变；在容量边界处选择压缩当前分配或获取额外物理页面，并与PagedAttention集成以保持连续批处理和前缀缓存。

Result: 在多个模型的推理基准测试中，GrowPage实现了优于现有方法的性能-吞吐量权衡。

Conclusion: 将KV容量视为运行时资源并进行动态管理，能有效提升大语言模型服务的效率。

Abstract: Long-output reasoning has made the key--value (KV) cache a critical memory bottleneck for efficient LLM serving. Existing KV compression methods usually rely on a predefined per-request budget and adjust only which KV states are retained, leaving the total capacity fixed throughout decoding. However, reasoning workloads exhibit substantial demand variation: different requests require different KV capacities, and the attention demand of an individual request evolves during generation. We introduce \textbf{GrowPage}, an on-demand KV budgeting framework that treats KV capacity as a runtime resource. GrowPage maintains lightweight dual-timescale query summaries to capture recent and long-term attention behaviors, and uses their relative attention working sets to estimate demand evolution. At each capacity boundary, GrowPage either compresses KV states within the current allocation or acquires an additional physical page when broader demand emerges. By integrating with PagedAttention's page-level memory abstraction, GrowPage preserves continuous batching and prefix caching. Experiments on reasoning benchmarks across multiple models show that GrowPage achieves a superior performance--throughput trade-off over existing approaches.

</details>


### [201] [PPO-STGNN: A Proximal Policy Optimization Approach with Spatio-Temporal Graph Neural Networks for DAG Task Scheduling in Cloud-Edge-End Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03503)
*Yangshuo Qi,Chenwei Wang,Zihan Shen,Songlin Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PPO-STGNN算法，结合近端策略优化（PPO）与时空图神经网络（STGNN），用于解决云-边-端协同环境中异构节点上计算密集型DAG任务调度的NP-hard问题，旨在最小化完成时间和调度长度比，同时提升资源负载均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着物联网发展，云-边-端环境中的DAG任务日益复杂且依赖性强。由于节点在计算能力、带宽和能耗上的高度异质性，传统启发式算法和常规强化学习方法难以捕捉系统资源的时空动态变化，导致调度效率低下。

Method: 1. 利用STGNN提取DAG任务拓扑与物理云-边-端资源图的时空特征；2. 采用PPO优化调度策略以最小化makespan和SLR，并改善CPU及内存负载均衡；3. 引入多教师行为克隆机制进行预训练，以加速模型收敛。

Result: 实验表明，PPO-STGNN在保持低完成时间的同时显著提升了负载均衡性能，有效解决了动态异构环境下的DAG调度难题。

Conclusion: 该算法适用于动态且异构的云-边-端DAG调度场景，为复杂依赖任务的资源分配提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid development of the Internet of Things, computation intensive directed acyclic graph (DAG) tasks have become increasingly common in cloud-edge-end collaborative environments. However, cloud, edge, and end nodes are highly heterogeneous in computing capacity, network bandwidth, and energy consumption, which makes the efficient scheduling of tasks with complex dependencies an NP-hard problem. Traditional heuristic algorithms and conventional reinforcement-learning methods often fail to capture the spatio-temporal dynamics of system resources. This paper proposes PPO-STGNN, a DAG task-scheduling algorithm that integrates proximal policy optimization (PPO) with spatio-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs). The method uses an STGNN to extract features from both the DAG task topology and the physical cloud-edge-end resource graph, and then optimizes the scheduling policy through PPO to minimize makespan and schedule length ratio (SLR) while improving CPU and memory load balancing. To accelerate convergence, a multi-teacher behavior-cloning mechanism is introduced for pretraining. Experimental results show that PPO-STGNN significantly improves load balancing while maintaining a low completion time, making it suitable for dynamic and heterogeneous cloud-edge- end DAG scheduling scenarios.

</details>


### [202] [What Matters for Aggressive Decoding-Time KV Eviction? Temporal Aggregation and Ranking Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03515)
*Bo Zeng,Yu Zhao,Yefeng Liu,Zhihong Lu,Xuanfan Ni,Xintong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在激进KV缓存压缩下，EMA聚合方式使得不同评分器的排序差异被掩盖。基于此，提出了InertiaKV及其惰性刷新变体以提升吞吐量，并验证了“无评分”解码策略的可行性，强调了时间聚合与排名保留是关键设计因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究过度关注Token评分函数的设计，而忽视了跨解码步骤聚合分数的时间规则这一实现细节。研究发现，在激进压缩下，EMA聚合导致评分器修改难以区分，需要探索更有效的 eviction 机制和聚合策略以平衡效率与质量。

Method: 1. 分析不同评分器（Value-norm, KeyDiff等）在EMA聚合下的表现，发现其稳定性源于层权重与时间保留的结合；2. 提出 InertiaKV（基于EMA的驱逐方法）和 InertiaKV-Lazy（周期性刷新变体）；3. 研究 Score-Free 解码策略（仅在首步评分并冻结排名）。

Result: 1. InertiaKV-Lazy 相比全刷新版本带来 1.34-1.46x 的解码吞吐量提升；2. Score-Free 策略平均质量变化仅为 +0.03，且移除了后续所有评分开销；3. 在六个开源骨干模型及 LongBench、RULER 等基准上验证了上述方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 时间聚合方式和排名保留是 KV 缓存压缩中 distinct 且关键的设计因素。虽然特定聚合方式可能掩盖评分器的细微差别，但这并不意味着评分质量在一般意义上无关紧要，而是提示需重新审视聚合策略对最终性能的影响。

Abstract: Decoding-time KV cache compression research focuses heavily on designing better token scoring functions, while the temporal rule that aggregates scores across decode steps is often treated as an implementation detail. Under aggressive KV compression, we find that exponential-moving-average (EMA) aggregation makes approximately order-preserving scorer modifications largely indistinguishable at the eviction-set level. Value-norm and entropy variants remain highly correlated with attention and produce nearly unchanged retention sets, whereas KeyDiff, key norm, recency, and a learned scorer alter the ranking and degrade substantially. We associate this stability with the evaluated aggregation, which couples layer weighting and temporal retention. Building on this observation, we introduce InertiaKV, an EMA-based decoding-time eviction method, and InertiaKV-Lazy, its periodic-refresh variant, which yields 1.34-1.46x decode throughput relative to full refresh InertiaKV. We also study Score-Free decoding as a separate empirical operating point: it scores the full context once at the first decode step, freezes that ranking, and incurs an average quality change of +0.03 while removing all subsequent scoring. Across six open-weight backbones and the LongBench, LongBench-v2, and RULER benchmarks, the results identify temporal aggregation and ranking preservation as distinct, consequential design factors; they do not imply that scoring quality is irrelevant in general.

</details>


### [203] [CulturalMenuBench: Probing the Knowledge-Application Gap in Multimodal Culinary Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03526)
*Bo Zeng,Linfeng Gao,Peiqin Lin,Yu Zhao,Mingyan Zeng,Yu Tong,Xintong Wang,Linlong Xu,Longyue Wang,Weihua Luo,Qinggang Zhang,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CulturalMenuBench基准测试，揭示多模态语言模型在食物识别上虽表现优异，但缺乏真正的文化理解能力，存在显著的“知识-应用”差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态模型在食物识别基准测试中得分接近上限，但其成功是源于真正的文化理解还是仅基于视觉匹配尚不明确，需探究这一区别。

Method: 构建包含10种语言、18个地区、4,870个样本的CulturalMenuBench基准，设计10项任务将最终菜品和分步烹饪图像与食材、程序文本及地区标签配对；评估12个模型并进行诊断分析和消融实验。

Result: 模型在标准多项选择题中准确率超94%，但在归因中国菜系时降至56%以下；错误模式符合随机猜测，准确率取决于视觉显著性而非文化结构，且通过菜名分类比通过图像更准确（高出7-18分）；消融实验证实任务确实需要程序性证据。

Conclusion: 完美的识别能力可能掩盖了应用文化知识能力的缺失，模型具备相关知识但无法通过视觉输入激活；需训练明确连接感知、程序和语境的模型。

Abstract: Multimodal language models achieve near-ceiling scores on food recognition benchmarks, yet it remains unclear whether this success reflects genuine cultural understanding or mere visual matching. To probe this distinction, we introduce CulturalMenuBench, a benchmark of 4,870 items in 10 languages across 18 regions; its 10 tasks pair final-dish and step-by-step cooking images with ingredients, procedural text, and regional labels, spanning basic recognition to process-grounded cultural attribution. Evaluating 12 models exposes a substantial knowledge-application gap: models exceeding 94% on standard multiple-choice tasks drop to at most 56% when attributing dishes to Chinese regional cuisines, despite an identical four-way format. Diagnostic analyses explain why: error patterns are consistent with random guessing, accuracy tracks visual distinctiveness rather than cultural structure, and models classify cuisines more accurately from dish names alone than from images (+7-18 points). The knowledge is thus present but cannot be activated through visual input. An ablation confirms these tasks genuinely require procedural evidence: removing sequential cooking images selectively degrades process-grounded tasks while others remain stable. Overall, CulturalMenuBench shows that near-perfect recognition can conceal an inability to apply cultural knowledge, motivating training that explicitly connects perception, procedure, and cultural context. Code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [204] [NeoRed: A Knowledge-Logic-Alignment Multimodal Large Language Model for Neonatal Respiratory Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03527)
*Yinan Liu,Hongtai Xia,Haoran Xu,Jiankang Hong,Jingkuan Song,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个针对新生儿呼吸疾病的模态大语言模型NeoRed，通过KLA框架解决领域差距和多维临床上下文整合不足的问题，在新生儿数据集上显著优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生儿呼吸系统疾病发病率和死亡率高，临床诊断挑战大。现有的多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）因训练数据主要基于成人且缺乏多维临床上下文的深度整合，导致在新生儿诊断中存在领域差距和准确性不足的问题。

Method: 1. 收集并发布了两个真实世界临床数据集：NeoCXR和NeoCXR-EV。
2. 提出了NeoRed模型，这是首个专为新生儿呼吸疾病设计的MLLM。
3. 设计了知识-逻辑-对齐（KLA）框架以增强异质临床上下文与胸部X光的联合诊断：
   - 知识先验注入（KPI）：将新生儿学家启发的诊断先验融入多模态表示，引导跨模态的疾病特定注意力。
   - 诊断逻辑约束（DLC）：使生成的报告语义与多模态诊断逻辑保持一致。
   - 视觉语义对齐（VSA）：建立视觉特征与成像结论之间的语义对应关系。

Result: 1. 在NeoCXR数据集上，NeoRed实现了准确的诊断报告生成，ROUGE-L得分为53.29%，临床疗效F1分数为65.19%，优于现有MLLMs。
2. NeoRed在成人基准测试（MIMIC-CXR和IU-Xray）上也保持了具有竞争力的报告生成性能。
3. 数据集将在申请后提供。

Conclusion: NeoRed通过引入特定的临床先验、逻辑约束和视觉语义对齐，有效解决了新生儿诊断中的领域适应和多模态融合问题，证明了其在提升新生儿呼吸系统疾病诊断报告生成准确性和专业性方面的有效性，同时保持了对成人数据的泛化能力。

Abstract: Neonatal respiratory diseases are a major cause of neonatal morbidity and mortality, posing substantial challenges in clinical practice. Despite recent advances, existing Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) face two key limitations in neonatal diagnosis: (1) domain gap arising from predominantly adult training data; (2) insufficient integration of multidimensional clinical context for accurate diagnosis. To address these challenges, we collect two real-world clinical datasets (NeoCXR and NeoCXR-EV) and propose NeoRed, to the best of our knowledge, the first MLLM tailored for neonatal respiratory disease, filling the gap in neonatal diagnostic reports generation. To enhance joint diagnosis from heterogeneous clinical context and chest X-rays, we design a novel Knowledge-Logic-Alignment (KLA) framework which constrains model behavior from three perspectives: 1) Knowledge Prior Injection (KPI) incorporates neonatologist-inspired diagnostic priors into multimodal representations, guiding disease-specific attention across modalities; 2) Diagnostic Logic Constraint (DLC) aligns the semantics of generated reports with multimodal diagnostic logic; and 3) Visual Semantic Alignment (VSA) establishes semantic correspondence between visual features and imaging conclusions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that NeoRed enables accurate neonatal diagnostic reports generation, achieving ROUGE-L of 53.29% and Clinical Efficacy F1 score of 65.19% on NeoCXR, outperforming existing MLLMs. NeoRed also preserves competitive report generation performance on adult benchmarks (MIMIC-CXR and IU-Xray). Datasets will be available upon application.

</details>


### [205] [Feature Reconfiguration With Visual Prior for Medical Lesion Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03535)
*Yinan Liu,Jiankang Hong,Zhen Gao,Ye Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FreNet，通过隐式先验神经网络(IPNN)和双域特征重配置(DFR)模块，在编码前和编码中分别进行像素级和特征级重配置，以解决医学图像病变分割中的背景干扰和形态多样问题，在9个基准上显著优于SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于编码器-解码器的方法缺乏编码阶段的早期先验引导和特征重配置，难以有效应对复杂背景干扰和多样的病变形态这两大挑战。

Method: 1. 构建FreNet框架，包含IPNN和DFR模块；2. IPNN利用SAM的视觉先验对输入图像进行编码前的像素级重配置以抑制背景响应；3. DFR模块在编码过程中通过频域解耦(FDM)增强前景-背景判别力，并通过空间定位模块(SLM)提升空间稳定性，实现特征级重配置。

Result: 在三个成像模态的9个医学图像分割基准上进行广泛实验，FreNet显著优于当前最先进(SOTA)方法；在具有挑战性的ETIS数据集上，Dice系数比SOTA方法提高5.0%，比SAM提高7.2%。

Conclusion: 提出的FreNet框架通过引入视觉先验和双域特征重配置机制，有效解决了医学病变分割中的关键难点，取得了优越的性能表现。

Abstract: Lesion segmentation in medical images plays a critical role in clinical diagnosis and treatment planning. Despite significant advances, lesion segmentation remains challenging due to two major factors: (1) complex background interference; (2) diverse lesion morphology. Existing encoder-decoder based methods mainly focus on enhancing feature extraction or redesigning decoding strategies. However, they lack early prior guidance and feature reconfiguration during the encoding stage, limiting their effectiveness in handling these challenges. To address these limitations, we propose FreNet, a feature reconfiguration framework with visual priors, which performs pixel-level reconfiguration before encoding and feature-level reconfiguration during encoding for precise medical lesion segmentation. To suppress background responses, we propose an Implicit Prior Neural Network (IPNN), which models a continuous spatial field and leverages visual prior from SAM to reconfigure input image before encoding stage. To better handle diverse lesion morphology, we design a Dual-domain Feature Reconfiguration (DFR) module to progressively reconfigure backbone features during encoding stage. Within DFR, the Frequency Decoupling Module (FDM) decouples backbone features in frequency domain to enhance foreground-background discriminability, while the Spatial Localization Module (SLM) spatially relocates and improving spatial stability after frequency decoupling. Extensive experiments on 9 medical image segmentation benchmarks across three imaging modalities demonstrate that FreNet significantly outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods. On the challenging ETIS dataset, our method achieves Dice improvements of 5.0% over SOTA method and 7.2% over SAM.

</details>


### [206] [Dalek: A Constructive Agent Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03546)
*Wanpeng Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dalek 是一种基于文本和消息代理的封闭机器，通过结合冯·诺依曼自复制自动机的核心与四项结构义务，实现了在任何符合主机合同的底物上的自我维护、进化、繁殖和组织能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一种具有自我维持、自我进化、自我繁殖和自我组织能力的通用封闭机器，使其能够在各种底物上运行并实现完整的遗传与演化闭环。

Method: 1. 基础构建：使用演员（actors）、消息（messages）和通道（channels）三个原语构建机器。
2. 结构约束：引入四项义务（主机边界、构造语言、允许转换、规则遗传性）确立机器的边界、身份和封闭性。
3. 核心融合：整合冯·诺依曼1948年自复制自动机的核心（自我描述、构造函数、拷贝函数、控制器）。
4. 载体实现：在文本和消息代理底物上重新推导介质，添加边界、身份、历史和生长的显式结构。
5. 能力扩展：利用大语言模型和编译器作为有效载荷，生成新能力并将其编译安装到自我描述中，由后代继承。

Result: Dalek 成功将大语言模型和编译器集成到其自我描述结构中，使得新能力的开发、编译、安装和遗传成为可能。该机器能够生成自身的器官甚至运行时环境，从而在机器内部闭合了遗传和演化的路径。

Conclusion: Dalek 证明了通过结合经典自复制理论、结构化约束和现代LLM技术，可以在通用计算底物上实现具备完整自我演化能力的封闭智能系统。

Abstract: We present Dalek, a closed machine designed for agents that realizes self-maintenance, self-evolution, self-reproduction, and self-organization on any substrate satisfying a general host contract. The machine is built from three primitives---actors, messages, and channels. Four obligations---a host boundary, a construction language, admissible transitions, and rule heredity---give its boundary, identity, and closure a structural basis.
  Von Neumann's 1948 self-reproducing automaton supplies a hereditary constructional core: a self-description together with a constructor, a copier, and a controller. Dalek combines this core with the four obligations and rederives its medium for a text-and-message agent substrate, adding explicit structures for boundary, identity, history, and growth. A large language model and a compiler occupy the payload position and form a general capability producer. New capabilities are authored, compiled, installed into the description, and inherited by descendants. The same path produces the machine's own organs and even its runtime, closing heredity and evolution within the machine.

</details>


### [207] [GPS-Bench: A Governance Policy Benchmark for Automating Policy Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03553)
*Linh Le,Melanie Bui,My Chiffon Nguyen,Zachary Schlosser,David Williams-King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GPS-Bench基准，通过连接立法记录等公共证据与政策、行为者及影响，评估多智能体模拟在政策分析中的有效性。研究发现微调优于分解，但分解提供了机制解释和可验证的联盟形成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型（LLM）的政策模拟缺乏有效性验证，因为通常未将模拟出的合理行为与实际观察到的结果进行对比。需要一种能够建立证据基础、识别受影响主体及其响应的分析方法。

Method: 构建GPS-Bench基准，利用立法记录、游说披露、监管文件等公开证据重构行为者（Persona as evidence object）。设立由人工标注组成的黄金测试集（Gold set）和由LLM基于检索证据生成的银级监督集（Silver supervision）。通过控制变量法对比联合推理、独立/通信智能体、基于图的方法和权重级微调等不同模式在单一政策状态下的表现。

Result: 在接地记录上进行微调在预测行为者层面的影响方面表现最强；单纯的分解方法并未超越微调的效果。然而，分解方法引入了机制解释能力：智能体持有私有且非相同的证据，针对具体合作伙伴提出联合提案，形成的联盟可与记录中的承诺进行比对验证。

Conclusion: GPS-Bench提供了一个共同的实证环境，证明了结合证据、行为者建模和多智能体交互可以显著提升对政策结果的预测准确性和可解释性，特别是在理解“为何共同行动优于单独行动”的机制方面具有独特价值。

Abstract: Policy analysis requires more than predicting whether a proposal will pass: it requires identifying who will be affected, how those actors respond, and what follows. LLM-based policy simulations model these processes at scale, but their validity is hard to establish when plausible behaviour is never compared with observed outcomes. We introduce GPS-Bench, an evidence-grounded benchmark for governance policy simulation that links policies to relevant actors, actor actions and downstream impacts using legislative records, lobbying disclosures, regulatory documents, corporate filings, economic data and other public evidence. Actors are reconstructed from the dated record rather than prompted as archetypes, so a persona is an evidence object with provenance; a human-annotated pool forms the Gold evaluation set, while cases labelled by a separate LLM from retrieved evidence are treated as Silver supervision and never as test labels. Because every inference mode reads the same grounded state and emits the same schema, GPS-Bench turns "does multi-agent simulation help?" into a controlled comparison: we contrast joint reasoning, independent and communicating actor agents, graph-based methods and weight-level fine-tuning over one policy state. Fine-tuning on the grounded record gives the strongest actor-level impact prediction, and decomposition does not beat it; what decomposition adds is mechanism. Agents hold private, non-identical evidence, each seeing its own exposure clause, and address named partners with concrete joint proposals, what they offer, what they need in return, and why acting together beats acting alone, so the coalitions that form can be checked against the commitments the record holds. GPS-Bench therefore gives a common empirical setting for studying when evidence, actor modelling and multi-agent interaction improve the prediction and interpretation of policy outcomes.

</details>


### [208] [HalluPeer: A Taxonomy-driven Benchmark for Detecting Hallucinations in Scientific Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03580)
*Tzu-Ling Lin,Dong-Ting Yao,Teng-Fang Hsiao,Wei-Chih Chen,Hong-Han Shuai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HalluPeer基准，用于检测科学同行评审中大语言模型生成的幻觉，解决现有基准不适配长技术论文验证的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着学术同行评审规模扩大，LLM被用作评审助手，但其可能生成流畅但无依据的主张，损害评审可靠性；现有幻觉基准未针对需基于长技术论文进行验证的同行评审场景设计。

Method: 构建HalluPeer基准，提供对齐的论文内容、人工撰写评审和注入幻觉的评审三元组；通过自动化过滤管道诱导同行评审特定幻觉分类法，识别评审上下文并注入幻觉，支持检测、分类和定位标注。

Result: 在1.2万篇论文和3.8万份评审上的实验显示，现有检测器难以区分幻觉与合法批评；真实评审评估表明HalluPeer定义的幻觉模式在实际中确实存在，凸显源感知验证的重要性。

Conclusion: 需要开发源感知的验证机制以应对同行评审中的幻觉问题，HalluPeer为相关研究和评估提供了基准支持。

Abstract: The growing scale of academic peer review has motivated the use of Large Language Models (LLMs) as review assistants, yet LLMs can generate fluent but unsupported claims that undermine review reliability. Existing hallucination benchmarks are not designed for peer review, where verification requires grounding claims in long, technical papers. We introduce HalluPeer, a benchmark for detecting hallucinations in scientific peer reviews, providing aligned triples of paper content, human-written reviews, and hallucination-injected reviews, annotated for detection, classification, and localization. Our pipeline induces a peer-review-specific hallucination taxonomy, identifies review contexts, and injects hallucinations with automated filtering. Experiments on 12K papers and 38K reviews show that existing detectors struggle to separate hallucinations from legitimate critique, while evaluation on authentic reviews demonstrates that HalluPeer-defined hallucination patterns occur in real peer reviews, highlighting the critical need for source-aware verification. Our project page can be found in https://github.com/Lin-TzuLing/HalluPeer.git

</details>


### [209] [The Attention Triangle in Audio-Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03586)
*Sagi Polaczek,Noa Kraicer,Gal Metzer,Zhuo Ning,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri,Daniel Cohen-Or,Raja Giryes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究音频-视频扩散模型中跨模态注意力导致的语义泄漏问题，揭示音频与视频间双向路由机制及其引发的偏差驱动错误，并提出利用注意力信号进行诊断和推理时干预以改善跨模态一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频-视频扩散模型依赖跨模态注意力协调文本、声音和视觉内容，但该机制可能引入微妙且系统的语义泄漏，导致生成内容与提示词意图不符。

Method: 分析连接文本、音频和视频流的“注意力三角”及跨模态信息路由；提取注意力衍生信号作为诊断工具以分析受控条件下的语义分布；利用这些信号引导推理时的干预措施以促进更一致的跨模态对齐。

Result: 发现音频-视频边具有双向影响特性，且受模型参数偏见主导；当提示词与学习到的先验冲突时，跨模态交互会覆盖预期条件，导致向视觉规范但错误的结果重定向；提出的干预方法能改善语义接地并保持生成质量。

Conclusion: 语义伪影源于特定路径上结构化的、由偏见驱动的跨模态交互，而非单纯的注意力扩散；通过诊断和干预跨模态路由，可以有效减轻语义泄漏并提升多模态一致性。

Abstract: Audio-video diffusion models rely on cross-modal attention to coordinate text, sound, and visual content, yet this same mechanism can introduce subtle and systematic semantic leakage. We study these models by probing and analyzing the ``attention triangle,'' comprising the three cross-attention edges connecting the text, audio, and video streams, and examine how semantic information is routed across modalities during generation. Our analysis reveals that routing along the audio-video edge is bidirectional: audio can influence video generation, while video can influence audio generation. This edge is shaped by biases encoded in the model's parameters and emerges as a major contributor to leakage: when prompts are in tension with learned priors, cross-modal interactions may override the intended conditioning and reroute semantics toward visually canonical but incorrect outcomes. These effects suggest that semantic artifacts arise not merely from attention spreading beyond its intended target, but from structured, bias-driven interactions along specific pathways. Building on this perspective, we extract attention-derived signals that expose how semantics are distributed and grounded across modalities, and use them as a diagnostic tool to both analyze and deliberately incur leakage under controlled conditions. This enables us to probe the internal dynamics of cross-modal routing and isolate the role of individual interactions. We further leverage these signals to guide inference-time interventions that encourage more consistent cross-modal alignment. Extensive experiments support our analysis and demonstrate improved semantic grounding while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [210] [KC-Bench: A Dynamic Interactive Benchmark for Evaluating Knowledge Conflicts in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03588)
*Yaxing Lyu,Shengjie Zhou,Binbin Toh,Pengyu Zhu,Lijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KC-Bench基准，用于评估LLM在工具使用中处理知识冲突、输入不一致和时序冲突的能力，发现现有模型在此方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地通过工具执行任务，它们必须在采取行动前协调用户指令、参数化知识和动态环境观察。现有的评估缺乏对这种复杂冲突处理能力（如事实修正、身份一致性检查和时序冲突解决）的系统性测量，导致潜在的错误传播风险。

Method: 构建了KC-Bench，这是一个受控的多轮基准测试，包含238个经过人工筛选的任务。该方法结合了用户模拟器、状态保持工具、确定性环境断言、开源自然语言评估器以及人类轨迹验证，旨在隔离并诊断模型层面的行为，而非评估完整的智能体框架。

Result: 对DeepSeek-V4-Flash、GLM-5.2和MiniMax-M3等九个模型的评估显示，存在显著的跨领域差异：没有任何模型能在所有设置下可靠地处理事实修正、身份一致性检查和时序冲突解决。在模拟环境中，未能识别的冲突可能会传播到工具调用或合成受保护数据流中。

Conclusion: KC-Bench为开发具有冲突感知推理和执行 safeguards 提供了可复现的诊断工具，强调了当前LLM在处理多源信息冲突方面的局限性，并为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: As LLMs increasingly act through tools, they must reconcile user instructions, parametric knowledge, and dynamic environmental observations before taking actions. We introduce KC-Bench, a controlled multi-turn benchmark for measuring this capability across world-knowledge conflicts, input inconsistencies, and multi-source temporal conflicts. Its 238 tasks are manually screened from more than 1,000 generated candidates and combine a user simulator, stateful tools, deterministic environment assertions, an open-source natural-language evaluator, and human trajectory verification. Evaluation of nine models, including DeepSeek-V4-Flash, GLM-5.2, and MiniMax-M3, shows substantial cross-domain variation: no model handles factual correction, identity consistency checking, and temporal conflict resolution reliably across all settings. In the simulated environments, missed conflicts can propagate to tool calls or synthetic protected-data flows. KC-Bench isolates this model-level behavior rather than ranking complete agent frameworks, and provides a reproducible diagnostic for developing conflict-aware reasoning and execution safeguards.

</details>


### [211] [A computable representation of the physical laboratory enables verifiable workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03621)
*Xiaobo Li,Luyao Ge,Xiaohui Li,Lulu Guo,Ming Mao,Jiwang Zheng,Wenting Guan,Xin Yang,Yi Luo,Jun Jiang,Linjiang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种通过类型化研究对象、能力绑定操作和组合式工作流代数来建立物理实验室的可计算表示，实现了代理推理与物理变换之间的通用计算接口，为端到端自主科学发现奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了使科学可计算，需要同时具备科学知识和其所受测试的物理世界的可计算表示。目前缺乏将机器可读知识与物理世界状态变化有效关联的通用框架。

Method: 1. 构建物理实验室的可计算表示：利用类型化研究对象、能力绑定操作及组合式工作流代数，将工作流表达为具有显式依赖、决策、迭代和并发性的程序。
2. 工程实现：在模块化智能机器人实验室中，将形式化操作绑定到可执行的“Function Skills”（功能技能）。
3. 仿真与验证：针对不同的科学意图生成能力相对的工作流，并通过状态仿真传播对象变换，在分发执行前验证操作前置条件和实验室约束。

Result: 成功建立了代理推理与能力绑定物理变换之间的通用计算接口；能够针对不同科学意图生成相应的工作流；通过仿真验证了操作的前置条件和实验室约束的有效性。

Conclusion: 该表示法及其工程框架共同构成了连接数字推理与物理实验操作的桥梁，为端到端的自主科学发现提供了坚实的基础设施和方法论支持。

Abstract: Making science computable requires representations of both scientific knowledge and the physical world in which scientific claims are tested. A computable representation of the physical laboratory is established through typed research objects, capability-bound operations and a compositional workflow algebra. It provides the physical-world counterpart to machine-readable knowledge, expressing workflows as programs over evolving laboratory states with explicit dependencies, decisions, iteration and concurrency. The representation was implemented in a modular agentic robotic laboratory by binding formal operations to executable Function Skills. For diverse scientific intents, capability-relative workflows were generated, while stateful simulation propagated object transformations and verified operation preconditions and laboratory constraints before dispatch. The proposed representation and its engineering framework jointly establish a general computational interface between agent reasoning and capability-bound physical transformations, providing a foundation for end-to-end autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [212] [Analysis of Prompt Engineering for Drug Toxicity Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03635)
*Mia MacGregor,Aakash Welgamage Don,Mark Bartlett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）在药物毒性预测中提示工程的作用，发现LLM的自然方差超过了提示微调的效果，而使用化学信息学代码提取特征能显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英国临床试验成本高昂且药物失败率高，毒性是主要因素之一。虽然LLMs被广泛用于预测药物毒性，但其对提示工程的敏感性存在争议，需探究提示措辞的重要性。

Method: 构建不同维度的提示（涉及角色、结构、规则解释）让LLM识别显著化学属性；利用LLM生成的特征构建数据集并输入机器学习算法；对比LLM生成值与化学信息学代码提取特征的效果。

Result: LLM的自然方差超过了提示微调带来的收益；使用化学信息学代码提取特征相比LLM生成值能带来模型性能的实质性提升。

Conclusion: 提出的分析方法适用于生物信息学领域的多种提示类型，表明在药物毒性预测中，传统化学信息学方法比依赖LLM生成特征更可靠。

Abstract: Clinical trials in the UK can cost up to £1.3 million, with approximately 90% drug failure rate. Toxicity is a major contributing factor in drug failure. Testing is time and cost intensive. In recent years, the use of artificial intelligence has been increasingly explored to aid in the prediction of drug toxicity, with extensive use of large language models (LLMs). However, LLMs can show considerable variation when minor changes are made to prompts, which raises concerns about their sensitivity to prompt engineering. Prompt engineering is used to optimise a prompt given to an LLM to generate the desired output. This paper proposes a method to analyse prompt engineering for drug toxicity prediction. The aim of the paper is to investigate the importance of prompt phrasing for drug toxicity prediction. LLMs were prompted to identify chemical properties of significance when predicting drug toxicity. Prompts were constructed to investigate; job role, prompt structuring, and rule interpretation.
